INSTALANDING.AI · Siliq AI Hardware Lab Page

The page below is the build. Use it as the starting point, not as inspiration:
- Keep its layout, design values (colours, type, spacing, radii) and motion exactly as they are.
- Replace the fictional brand, copy, names and numbers with mine.
- If I name a stack (React, Next.js, Vue, Svelte, Astro...), convert it faithfully to that stack;
  otherwise ship it as this one HTML file.
- Read the MAP and ADAPTING notes in its header comment before you edit.
- Check it in a real browser: no console errors, no horizontal scroll at 390px, reduced motion respected.

Credit: keep this line as a comment at the top of the file you produce:
Siliq AI Hardware Lab Page · INSTALANDING.AI (MIT) · https://instalanding.ai/prompt/siliq-ai-hardware-lab-page

siliq-lab.html:

<!doctype html>
<!--
  INSTALANDING.AI · Siliq AI Hardware Lab Page
  slug     siliq-ai-hardware-lab-page
  prompt   https://instalanding.ai/p/siliq-ai-hardware-lab-page.txt   (the one-shot brief this file was built from)
  page     https://instalanding.ai/prompt/siliq-ai-hardware-lab-page
  license  MIT. Fictional business; every name, price, address and review is invented. Replace them before shipping.

  MAP
  tokens    :root at the top of <style>: ground, paper, sky, honey, tangerine, leaf and ink colours; fonts (display
            Climate Crisis, text Google Sans, hand Playpen Sans); spacing, radii and a pop ease. .tone-* classes
            set each tile's colours.
  sections  nav, hero, story, products, school, process, gallery, team, reviews, pricing, faq, contact, footer
  scripts   inline: menu sheet, anchor scrolling, sticky stages, the pricing switch, review marquee, form. Then
            GSAP 3.13 + ScrollTrigger + SplitText + Lenis 1.3 (jsdelivr) for the scroll scenes: the hero board
            breaking apart, pop/drop/slide reveals, split-line story text, the step timeline, the gallery collapsing
            into a grid, pricing cards fanning out and the footer wordmark.
  assets    none: every product, the lab and the portraits are inline SVG symbols in the sprite at the top of <body>;
            fonts from Google Fonts

  ADAPTING
  - Big fitted text sizes itself from --len (its character count) and the container width; update --len with the text.
  - html.motion is added in <head> only when motion is allowed and removed if GSAP has not loaded in time; every
    hidden-until-animated state is scoped under .motion, so the page is complete without it.
  - Swap a picture by pointing <use href> at another symbol; the portraits are large, so delete unused ones.
-->

.ibtn{width:52px;height:52px;background:var(--honey)}.contact-form{display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:14px;padding:var(--pad)}.contact-form h3{font-size:clamp(26px, 2.6vw, textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:var(--leaf);background:var(--paper)}.contact-form .btn{align-self:flex-start;margin-top:6px}.form-note{font-size:13px;color:var(--ink-soft)}.contact-form.is-sent .btn{--b-bg:var(--sky);--b-fg:var(--ink)}@media .fit{--fit-w:100cqi;margin-inline:auto;line-height:.86}.foot-cell{display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;padding:var(--pad);border-radius:var(--r-tile);background:var(--paper);min-height:150px}.foot-cell h2{font:600 13px/1 var(--font-text);color:var(--ink-soft)}.foot-cell ul{display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:flex-start;gap:8px}.foot-cell .hand{font-size:clamp(20px, 1.8vw, 26px)}.foot-cell.tone-sky,.foot-cell.tone-honey{background:var(--t-bg)}.foot-legal{grid-column:1 / -1;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px 24px;padding:14px 6px 0;font-size:14px;color:var(--ink-soft)}@media (max-width:899px){.foot-board{grid-template-columns:repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr))}.foot-cell{min-height:0}}@media (max-width:479px){.foot-board{grid-template-columns:minmax(0, 1fr)}}.motion .hero::after{content:"";display:block;height:110svh}.motion [data-stick]{position:sticky;top:var(--stick, 0px)}.motion .pre{visibility:hidden}.motion .flood{display:block;position:absolute;z-index:2;inset:0;background:var(--honey);clip-path:inset(50% 50% 50% 50%);pointer-events:none}.spin{transform-box:fill-box;transform-origin:center;animation:spin 2.4s linear infinite}@keyframes spin{to{transform:rotate(1turn)}}.blink{animation:blink 1.6s steps(2) infinite}@keyframes blink{50%{opacity:.25}}.float{animation:floaty 3s ease-in-out infinite}@keyframes floaty{50%{transform:translateY(-6px);opacity:.35}}.trace{stroke-dasharray:14 8;animation:trace 1.2s linear infinite}@keyframes trace{to{stroke-dashoffset:-44}}@media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){*,*::before,*::after{animation:none !important;transition:none !important}}</style>
</head>
<body>
<!-- Illustration sprite: every picture on the page is drawn in SVG -->
<svg width="0" height="0" style="position:absolute" aria-hidden="true" focusable="false">
  <defs>
    <linearGradient id="g-metal" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fbfdfd"/><stop offset=".45" stop-color="#c6cfd3"/><stop offset=".56" stop-color="#a4afb5"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#e4eaed"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-brushed" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#eef2f3"/><stop offset=".3" stop-color="#b9c3c8"/><stop offset=".52" stop-color="#f6f8f9"/><stop offset=".78" stop-color="#a1acb2"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#dbe1e4"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-dark" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#34474f"/><stop offset=".5" stop-color="#17242a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#091115"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-unit" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#2b3c43"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#121c21"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-gold" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff2bf"/><stop offset=".35" stop-color="#f6c24c"/><stop offset=".68" stop-color="#c4851a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#ffdd7c"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-gold-v" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff2bf"/><stop offset=".4" stop-color="#f6c24c"/><stop offset=".75" stop-color="#c4851a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#ffdd7c"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-pcb" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#16a07a"/><stop offset=".55" stop-color="#0a5c46"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#04301f"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-irid" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#6a4cff"/><stop offset=".28" stop-color="#1fa3d6"/><stop offset=".52" stop-color="#3ce2b2"/><stop offset=".78" stop-color="#ffd45c"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#ff7ab0"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-orange" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ffb877"/><stop offset=".5" stop-color="#f47a3c"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#b9461a"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-honey" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ffe394"/><stop offset=".5" stop-color="#ffbf3c"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#d48c16"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-rgb" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="0"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ff6a3c"/><stop offset=".33" stop-color="#ffd45c"/><stop offset=".66" stop-color="#3ce2b2"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#7cc4ea"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-blade" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#e6f5fd" stop-opacity=".95"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#5fa9d3" stop-opacity=".6"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-shine" x1="0" y1="0" x2="1" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity=".7"/><stop offset=".4" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity=".08"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity="0"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-warm" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff1c4"/><stop offset=".55" stop-color="#ffc657"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#d07a1c"/></linearGradient>
    <linearGradient id="g-wood" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#ffd27a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#d48c16"/></linearGradient>
    <radialGradient id="g-knob" cx=".35" cy=".3" r=".85"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff"/><stop offset=".45" stop-color="#c9d2d6"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#5f6c72"/></radialGradient>
    <radialGradient id="g-led-a"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fffbe6"/><stop offset=".45" stop-color="#ffcf4a"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#ff9a1f" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient>
    <radialGradient id="g-led-c"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#f0ffff"/><stop offset=".45" stop-color="#7cdcff"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#2aa9e0" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient>
    <radialGradient id="g-shadow"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#06141a" stop-opacity=".42"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#06141a" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient>
    <radialGradient id="g-sheen" cx=".36" cy=".28" r=".75"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity=".45"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#fff" stop-opacity="0"/></radialGradient>
    <radialGradient id="g-screen" cx=".5" cy=".5" r=".7"><stop offset="0" stop-color="#0e3a2e"/><stop offset="1" stop-color="#020b08"/></radialGradient>
    <filter id="f-drop" x="-25%" y="-25%" width="150%" height="160%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="7" stdDeviation="6" flood-color="#06141a" flood-opacity=".38"/></filter>
    <filter id="f-lift" x="-20%" y="-20%" width="140%" height="150%"><feDropShadow dx="0" dy="2" stdDeviation="1.6" flood-color="#06141a" flood-opacity=".45"/></filter>
    <filter id="f-glow" x="-60%" y="-60%" width="220%" height="220%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="2.4" result="b"/><feMerge><feMergeNode in="b"/><feMergeNode in="b"/><feMergeNode in="SourceGraphic"/></feMerge></filter>
    <filter id="f-haze" x="-50%" y="-50%" width="200%" height="200%"><feGaussianBlur stdDeviation="14"/></filter>
    <clipPath id="c-chiplid"><rect x="56" y="56" width="88" height="88" rx="8"/></clipPath>
    <clipPath id="c-soc"><rect x="76" y="78" width="48" height="48" rx="4"/></clipPath>
    <clipPath id="c-screen"><rect x="28" y="32" width="196" height="140" rx="8"/></clipPath>

    <svg viewBox="0 0 48 48" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M24 2C40 2 46 8 46 24S40 46 24 46 2 40 2 24 8 2 24 2Z" fill="var(--honey)"/><path d="M29 12.5c-6.5-3-13.5 1-10.5 6.6s12.5 2.6 10.2 9.8c-1.6 5-8 6.4-12.4 3.2" fill="none" stroke="var(--leaf)" stroke-width="6" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>
    <span class="brand-name">Siliq</span>
  </a>

  <div class="sheet">
    <a class="tone-sky" href="#menu">Lineup</a>
    <a class="tone-honey" href="#school">School</a>
    <a class="tone-tangerine" href="#pricing">Compute club</a>

  </div>
</header>

<main>
  <section id="hero" class="hero" data-kind="hero" data-section="hero">
    <div class="hero-stage" data-stick>
      <div class="board" data-fixed="5">
        <article class="tile t-main">
          <h1 class="display main-title"><span class="fit" style="--len:5">Built</span><span class="fit" style="--len:6">today!</span></h1>
          <div class="main-art">
            <figure class="main-photo"><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="AI accelerator chip"><use href="#i-chip"/></svg></figure>
            <svg class="sparks" viewBox="0 0 48 48" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M24 5v10M8 13l7 7M40 13l-7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg>
            <p class="sticker"><small>from</small><b>$24</b></p>
          </div>
          <div class="main-foot">
            <p class="main-note">Small-batch accelerators, dev boards and inference servers, flashed and tested every morning at five.</p>
            <div class="main-actions"><a class="btn" href="#contact">Order a dev kit</a><a class="btn btn--paper" href="#menu">See the lineup</a></div>
          </div>
        </article>
        <a class="tile t-feature" href="#menu">
          <h2 class="display feat-name">Edge NPU</h2>
          <div class="art tone-white"><span class="blob"></span><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="USB neural accelerator stick"><use href="#i-stick"/></svg><svg class="sparks" viewBox="0 0 48 48" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M24 5v10M8 13l7 7M40 13l-7 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="5" stroke-linecap="round"/></svg></div>
          <p class="feat-price">$129</p>
          <p class="feat-desc">Palm-sized accelerator with 40 TOPS and a silent passive heatsink</p>
        </a>
        <div class="tile t-rating">
          <p class="rate" aria-label="Rated 4.8"><span class="br" aria-hidden="true">(</span><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:3">4.8</b><span class="br" aria-hidden="true">)</span></p>
          <p class="rate-note">stars from 1,200 builders</p>
        </div>
        <a class="tile t-picks" href="#menu">
          <div class="picks-top"><h2 class="display">Top picks</h2><p>The four parts you ordered most this week.</p></div>
          <ul class="thumbs">
            <li><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="Inference chip"><use href="#i-chip"/></svg></li>
            <li><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 240 170" role="img" aria-label="GPU card"><use href="#i-gpu"/></svg></li>
            <li><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 240 110" role="img" aria-label="Memory module"><use href="#i-ram"/></svg></li>
            <li><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="Cooling fan"><use href="#i-fan"/></svg></li>
          </ul>
        </a>
        <a class="tile t-school" href="#school">
          <h2 class="display fit" style="--len:9">Workshops</h2>
          <p>Learn to build with our lab crew</p>
          <svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="Robot arm holding a chip"><use href="#i-arm"/></svg>
          <span class="join hand">Join in!<svg viewBox="0 0 64 48" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M4 6c8 22 24 30 50 30" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-dasharray="4 6"/><path d="M46 28l10 8-11 6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg></span>
        </a>
      </div>
      <div class="flood" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section id="story" class="story" data-kind="manifesto" data-section="story">
    <div class="wrap">
      <p class="story-text" data-split>We solder by hand, test in small batches <span class="inl"><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="Circuit board"><use href="#i-board"/></svg></span> and ship before noon. No shortcuts, no throttling <span class="inl r"><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="Chip"><use href="#i-chip"/></svg></span> just copper, silicon and a very long burn-in <span class="inl"><svg class="pic" viewBox="0 0 200 200" role="img" aria-label="Spinning fan"><use href="#i-fan"/></svg></span></p>
      <p class="story-sign hand" data-reveal>— Nell Hart, lead engineer</p>
      <ul class="facts">
        <li class="fact" data-pop><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:4">2015</b><p>First board out of the reflow oven on Foundry Row</p></li>
        <li class="fact" data-pop><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:2">36</b><p>Different modules on the shelf every day</p></li>
        <li class="fact" data-pop><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:4">48 h</b><p>Burn-in at full load before anything ships</p></li>
      </ul>
    </div>
  </section>

          <li class="class-row" data-slide><p class="date"><b>21</b><span>Nov</span></p><div class="class-info"><h3>Run LLMs on the edge</h3><p>3 hours · 10 seats left</p></div><b class="class-price">$80</b><a class="ibtn" href="#contact" aria-label="Book: Run LLMs on the edge"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M5 12h13M13 6l6 6-6 6" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2.4" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg></a></li>

        </ul>
        <div class="private"><p><strong>Private session?</strong> Teams, hackathons and offsites get the whole bench and a dev kit each to take home.</p><a class="lnk" href="#contact">Ask for a date</a></div>

  <section id="steps" class="steps" data-kind="process" data-section="process">
    <div class="wrap">
      <div class="sec-head">
        <h2 class="display h2" data-reveal>From wafer to rack</h2>
        <div class="sec-aside" data-reveal><p class="lead">A day at the lab starts in the dark and ends when the last unit passes burn-in.</p></div>
      </div>
      <div class="steps-track">
        <div class="steps-line" aria-hidden="true"><svg viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M8 5l8 7-8 7" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="3.5" stroke-linecap="round" stroke-linejoin="round"/></svg></div>
        <ol class="step-list">
          <li class="step tone-white"><span class="dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:4">5:00</b><h3>Reflow on</h3><p>The first panels roll through the oven while the street is still asleep.</p></li>
          <li class="step tone-honey"><span class="dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:4">7:30</b><h3>Bench tested</h3><p>Every board boots, benchmarks and logs its temperatures.</p></li>
          <li class="step tone-sky"><span class="dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:4">9:00</b><h3>Bikes out</h3><p>Local orders leave on cargo bikes; everything else ships overnight.</p></li>
          <li class="step tone-tangerine"><span class="dot" aria-hidden="true"></span><b class="display fit fig" style="--len:5">12:00</b><h3>Batch closed</h3><p>Spare boards go to the school makerspace down the road.</p></li>
        </ol>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

        <p>average from 1,200 reviews by builders and labs</p>

        <h2 class="display h2" data-reveal>Heard on the forum</h2>
        <p class="lead" data-reveal>Our builders write the best copy. We only fixed the typos.</p>

    </div>

      <div class="marquee-track">
        <ul class="marquee-list">
          <li class="review tone-white"><p class="quote">The NPU stick ruined every other accelerator for me. It runs my home cameras offline, all day, on a few watts.</p><p class="who"><b>Priya S.</b><span>builder since 2019</span></p></li>
          <li class="review tone-sky"><p class="quote">We ordered a custom inference box two weeks ahead and it was the fastest thing in the office. Including the intern.</p><p class="who"><b>Martin K.</b><span>ordered a custom build</span></p></li>
          <li class="review tone-honey"><p class="quote">The home AI server class was the most relaxing Saturday I've had all year. My server is called Nell now.</p><p class="who"><b>Hannah L.</b><span>hardware school</span></p></li>
          <li class="review tone-white"><p class="quote">Next-day swap-ins on the office rack are the only reason our demo day went ahead.</p><p class="who"><b>Dev A.</b><span>Office rack member</span></p></li>
          <li class="review tone-tangerine"><p class="quote">Sensible docs, fast shipping, and Joe answered my driver question in four minutes.</p><p class="who"><b>Clara B.</b><span>Foundry Row neighbour</span></p></li>
          <li class="review tone-sky"><p class="quote">A fanless board running a 7B model and nobody at the meetup believed it. We checked twice.</p><p class="who"><b>Sam O.</b><span>builder since 2022</span></p></li>
        </ul>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

  <section id="faq" class="faq" data-kind="faq" data-section="faq">
    <div class="wrap faq-grid">
      <div class="tile faq-side tone-sky">
        <h2 class="display h2">Good questions</h2>
        <p class="lead">The things people ask at the front desk while their board is being flashed.</p>
        <p class="hand faq-hand">Still stuck? Ask Joe!</p>
        <a class="btn btn--paper" href="#contact">Ask us anything</a>
      </div>
      <div class="faq-list">
        <details class="qa" data-reveal><summary><span>Will it work with my ML framework?</span><i class="plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></summary><div class="qa-a"><p>Out of the box: PyTorch, ONNX and the common runtimes, with drivers pre-flashed and tested. Tell us your stack and we will check compatibility before you order.</p></div></details>
        <details class="qa" data-reveal><summary><span>How late can I order?</span><i class="plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></summary><div class="qa-a"><p>Until 9 pm for next-morning dispatch. Custom builds and anything laser-engraved need a week.</p></div></details>
        <details class="qa" data-reveal><summary><span>Do you deliver?</span><i class="plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></summary><div class="qa-a"><p>Our cargo bikes cover two miles around Foundry Row from 9 am, Tuesday to Saturday, and everything else ships overnight. Delivery is free for Compute club members and $9 for everyone else.</p></div></details>
        <details class="qa" data-reveal><summary><span>Can I order a custom build?</span><i class="plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></summary><div class="qa-a"><p>Yes, with a week's notice. Send us the workload, the budget and one wild idea; we will come back with a parts list and a price.</p></div></details>
        <details class="qa" data-reveal><summary><span>Are classes okay for complete beginners?</span><i class="plus" aria-hidden="true"></i></summary><div class="qa-a"><p>Absolutely. Most people at the bench have never held a soldering iron. You get safety glasses, a printed guide and as much patience as you need.</p></div></details>
      </div>
    </div>
  </section>

        <label>What should we build?<textarea name="message" rows="4"></textarea></label>
        <button class="btn btn--leaf" type="submit" data-sent="Thanks! We'll reply today"><span class="btn-label">Send the order</span></button>
        <p class="form-note">We reply within a few hours, from 9 am to 6 pm.</p>
      </form>

  <div class="foot-board">
    <div class="foot-word"><p class="display fit" style="--len:5">Siliq</p></div>
    <div class="foot-cell tone-sky"><p class="hand">Hardware lab &amp; school on Foundry Row since 2015</p><a class="lnk" href="#hero">Back to the top</a></div>
    <div class="foot-cell"><h2>Visit</h2><ul><li>214 Foundry Row, Portland</li><li>Tue–Sun, 9:00–18:00</li></ul></div>
    <div class="foot-cell"><h2>Follow</h2><ul><li><a class="lnk" href="#contact">GitHub</a></li><li><a class="lnk" href="#contact">Newsletter</a></li></ul></div>
    <div class="foot-cell tone-honey"><h2>Pre-order</h2><a class="btn" href="#contact">Order a dev kit</a></div>
    <div class="foot-legal"><p>© 2026 Siliq Labs Inc. A fictional business made for a template demo.</p><p>Made with copper, silicon and a very long burn-in.</p></div>
